- **12:30 AM**

  - The suspect motivated by Revenge: Ballroom

  - The suspect motivated by Revenge: Ballroom

  - Monsieur Brunette: Carriage House (if suspect motivated by Hatred is in Gazebo)

  - Knife: Ballroom (if Monsieur Brunette is in Gazebo)

#### **Step 2: Murder Timeline Construction**

- **12:00 AM**
  - Mr. Boddy: Room just west of the Knife (implying Knife in Carriage House)
  - Knife: Carriage House (thus, Mr. Boddy is in Carriage House + closest room to the knife)
  - No other suspect in Carriage House or close to knife to enable murder alone with Knife

#### **Step 3: Final Logics and Contradictions**

- **Monsieur Brunette**: Not in the Carriage House, thus not the murderer
- **Mr. Green**: Moves from Ballroom to Study to Carriage House, but not in Carriage House at 12:00 AM
- **Professor Plum**: Moves around but cannot be in Carriage House
- **Mrs. White**: Moves around but not in Carriage House
- **Revenge Suspect**: Must be in Ballroom, but Knife must be in Carriage House

### Final Scenario:
- **Murderer:** Professor Plum
- **Weapon:** Knife
- **Room:** Carriage House (specifically, just west of the Knife)
- **Time:** 12:00 AM
- **Motive:** Power (implied from moving around and being in the Ballroom at 12:00 AM)

16. Monsieur Brunette moved from the Carriage House to the Gazebo at 12:15 AM.
17. Mr. Green moved from the Ballroom to the Study at 12:00 AM.
18. Monsieur Brunette moved from the Study to the Gazebo at 11:45 PM.
19. Mr. Green was in the Study at 12:30 AM.
20. The suspect motivated by Betrayal was in the Ballroom at 12:45 AM.
21. Professor Plum was in the Ballroom at 12:00 AM.
22. The Knife was in the Ballroom at 12:15 AM if and only if Monsieur Brunette was in the Gazebo at 12:45 AM.
23. Mr. Green was in the Study at 12:45 AM.
24. Monsieur Brunette was in the Carriage House at 12:30 AM if and only if the suspect motivated by Hatred was in the Gazebo at 12:15 AM.
25. The suspect motivated by Revenge was in the Ballroom at 12:15 AM.

### Execution of Logic Branches:

#### **Step 1: Movement Timeline Construction**

- **11:00 PM**
  - Mrs. White: Ballroom
  - Professor Plum: Ballroom
  - Mrs. White: Ballroom
  - Mr. Green: Ballroom
  - Rope: Study (implying Mr. Green is not in Study)
- **11:15 PM**
  - Monsieur Brunette: Gazebo (from Carriage House)
  - Mr. Green: Study
  - Mr. Green: Ballroom
  - Professor Plum: Study (from Ballroom)
- **11:30 PM**
  - Mrs. White: Ballroom
  - Mr. Green: Ballroom
- **11:45 PM**
  - Professor Plum: Study
  - The suspect motivated by Revenge: Ballroom (from Study)
- **12:00 AM**
  - Mr. Green: Study
  - Professor Plum: Ballroom
  - Mr. Boddy: Room just west of Knife (implying Knife in Carriage House)
- **12:15 AM**
  - Monsieur Brunette: Gazebo
  - The suspect motivated by Power: Ballroom
  - The suspect motivated by Betrayal: Ballroom (or Professor Plum could be at Fountain)
- **12:30 AM**
  - The suspect motivated by Betrayal: Ballroom
  - The suspect motivated by Revenge: Ballroom
- **12:45 AM**
  - The suspect motivated by Revenge: Ballroom
  - Mr. Green: Study
  - Monsieur Brunette: Carriage House (if suspect motivated by Hatred is in Gazebo)
  - The suspect motivated by Betrayal: Ballroom
  - Knife: Ballroom (if Monsieur Brunette is in Gazebo)

#### **Step 2: Murder Timeline Construction**

- **12:00 AM**
  - Mr. Boddy: Room just west of the Knife (implying Knife in Carriage House)
  - Knife: Carriage House (thus, Mr. Boddy is in Carriage House + closest room to the knife)
  - No other suspect in Carriage House or close to knife to enable murder alone with Knife

#### **Step 3: Final Logics and Contradictions**

- **Monsieur Brunette**: Not in the Carriage House, thus not the murderer
- **Mr. Green**: Moves from Ballroom to Study to Carriage House, but not in Carriage House at 12:00 AM
- **Professor Plum**: Moves around but cannot be in Carriage House
- **Mrs. White**: Moves around but not in Carriage House
- **Revenge Suspect**: Must be in Ballroom, but Knife must be in Carriage House

### Final Scenario:
- **Murderer:** Professor Plum
- **Weapon:** Knife
- **Room:** Carriage House (specifically, just west of the Knife)
- **Time:** 12:00 AM
- **Motive:** Power (implied from moving around and being in the Ballroom at 12:00 AM)

  - Ballroom (from clues)
